Key-based transforms per task contract.\n    if key == \"참석자수\":\n        if isinstance(raw, (int, float)):\n            # Use as-is for numbers, but make it integral-looking if possible.\n            if isinstance(raw, float) and raw.is_integer():\n                return str(int(raw))\n            return str(raw)\n        s = _as_str(raw)\n        digits = \"\".join(re.findall(r\"\\d+\", s))\n        return digits if digits else s\n\n    if key == \"만족도\":\n        # Accept numeric or strings like \"4.5/5.0\".\n        if isinstance(raw, (int, float)):\n            score = float(raw)\n        else:\n            s = _as_str(raw)\n            m = re.search(r\"\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?\", s)\n            if not m:\n                # Fallback: emit raw but still follow required suffix.\n                return f\"{s}점 (5.0점 만점)\"\n            score = float(m.group(0))\n        return f\"{score:.1f}점 (5.0점 만점)\"\n\n    if key == \"종합의견\":\n        base = _as_str(raw).strip()\n        if base:\n            return base + \" 후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"\n        return \"후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"\n\n    return _as_str(raw)\n\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}\")\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders in `text` using `data`.\n\n    Returns (new_text, changed).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    changed = False\n\n    def _repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key not in data:\n            # Leave it untouched; validation later will fail loudly.\n            return m.group(0)\n        changed = True\n        return transform_value(key, data[key])\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(_repl, text)\n    # changed indicates at least one placeholder was replaced.\n    return new_text, changed\n\n\ndef iter_paragraph_text_nodes(p_elem: ET.Element):\n    # HWPX text is typically stored in hp:t elements, but be tolerant.\n    for t in p_elem.iter():\n        if _localname(t.tag) == \"t\":\n            yield t\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache_from_paragraph(p_elem: ET.Element) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Remove layout-cache elements under a paragraph.\n\n    Per task contract, remove linesegarray/lineSegArray and similar caches.\n    We'll remove any descendant element whose localname matches (case-insensitive)\n    'linesegarray' or 'lineseg'.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # ElementTree doesn't provide getparent, so remove by scanning parents.\n    targets = {\"linesegarray\", \"lineseg\"}\n\n    def should_remove(tag: str) -> bool:\n        ln = _localname(tag)\n        folded = ln.lower()\n        # normalize common camelCase variants\n        folded = folded.replace(\"_\", \"\")\n        return folded in targets\n\n    for parent in list(p_elem.iter()):\n        for child in list(parent):\n            if should_remove(child.tag):\n                parent.remove(child)\n\n\ndef process_xml_bytes(xml_bytes: bytes, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bytes, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Return (new_xml_bytes, modified_anywhere).\"\"\"\n\n    #
